Fast full conv:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    M = 2*n - 1
    M_fft = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M_fft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, M_fft)[:M]
```
For real product. Numerical tiny negative possible; ignore? For nonnegative conv positive but FFT error. Use `np.maximum(c,0)` maybe for max, but gradient uses raw? For score max, max with no negatives. We can clip c to 0 for max to avoid negative spikes. But for LSE, small negatives okay? Could clip c = np.maximum(c,0) in objective. Gradient formula with p based on clipped not exact but fine. We'll do `c = np.maximum(fast_conv(x), 0.0)`.

Score:
```
def score_full(a):
    n=len(a); c=fast_conv(a); S=a.sum(); return 2*n*c.max()/S^2 if S>0
```

LSE full objective top-level:
```
def lse_full_objective(x, beta):
    n = len(x)
    a = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    S = a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-300: return 1e300, np.zeros_like(x)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shifted = beta*(c - cmax)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700, 700)
    exp_shifted = np.exp(shifted)
    z = exp_shifted.sum()
    if z <= 0: ...
    L = cmax + np.log(z)/beta
    p = exp_shifted / z
    # gradient wrt a = 2*conv(a, p_rev) selection
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    M3 = (2*n - 1) + n - 1
    M3_fft = 1 << (M3 - 1).bit_length()
    PA = np.fft.rfft(a, M3_fft)
    PR = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, M3_fft)
    conv_ap = np.fft.irfft(PA * PR, M3_fft)[:M3]
    # j index: conv[a, p_rev][2n-2-j]
    # Create h in order j=0..n-1: indices reversed from 2n-2 down to n-1?
    h = conv_ap[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]? Need check.
```
Index: conv length 3n-2, index `2n-2 - j` for j=0..n-1. For j=0 index 2n-2. j=n-1 index n-1. So slice start=2n-2, stop=n-1? Using negative stop exclusive? `conv_ap[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]` gives indices 2n-2 down to n-1 inclusive? In Python, start 2n-2, stop n-2, step -1 => last index > n-2, i.e. n-1. Count n. Good. For n=2, start2 stop0 step-1 -> indices2,1? length2. conv a,p_rev length4 indices 0,1,2,3; h0=2, h1=1 correct. For n=1, start0 stop -1? works? n likely >1.
    grad_L = 2.0 * h
    score = 2.0*n*L/(S*S)
    grad = (2.0*n)/(S*S) * grad_L - (4.0*n*L)/(S**3) * np.ones(n)
    return score, grad
```
But L-BFGS with bounds [0,1000] and objective includes scaling. Need maybe multiply objective by constant? Fine.

def run_lbfgs(x0, beta, maxiter=1000, maxfun=20000, m=10, tol...):
    x = normalize(x0.copy())
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * len(x)
    try:
        res = minimize(lse_full_objective, x, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=...)
    ...
```

Implement `lse_norm_objective(x, beta)`:
```
a = np.maximum(x,0)
S=a.sum(); if S<... return inf, zeros
an = a/S
c=fast_conv(an); L, p as before; grad_a=...
grad_x = (grad_a - an*float(np.dot(an, grad_a)))/S
return 2n*L, grad_x  # S=1 score
```

    # Estimate one objective timing

    # L-BFGS each iteration maybe 4-10 fun, so iterations per sec = 1/(dt*6)
    est_iter = int(time_limit / max(dt*6, 1e-6))
    maxiter = max(100, min(maxiter_cap, est_iter))

    ...
```

   a=normalize; best

       M=c.max(); sc=2nM
       update
       # choose top K indices by c
       top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:] if top_k<len(c)
       weights= c[top_idx] - M + 1e-12? maybe exp(beta*(c-M))
       if use single max: top_idx=[argmax]
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       for k in top_idx:
          w = weights[m]
          # derivative of c_k: for j where 0 <= k-j < n
          j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(k,n-1)
          vals = a[j_start:j_end+1][::-1]  # a[k-j]
          grad[j_start:j_end+1] += 2*w*vals

       # If weights not normalized, scale: If Q=∑ p_k c_k with p sum1, grad_Q =∑ p grad(c_k). For max, use p sum1. So compute weights = softmax over top_k; grad = ∑ p 2*a_{k-j}; subgrad score =2n*grad (since S=1). Good.

       # clip and normalize; maybe line search try a few step fractions
```

def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
    if len(a)==new_n: return a.copy()

LSE objective functions top-level. Need two objectives for scipy: full unnormalized? We'll implement normalized for better scaling:
```
def lse_norm_objective(x, beta):
    a = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    S = a.sum()
    if S < 1e-300: return 1e300, np.zeros_like(x)
    an = a / S
    c = fast_conv(an)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shifted = np.clip(beta*(c-cmax), -700, 700)
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    z = exps.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(z) or z<=0: return 1e300, zeros
    L = cmax + np.log(z)/beta
    p = exps / z
    # derivative of L wrt an: 2*conv(an, p_rev) selected
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    M3 = 3*n - 2
    M3_fft = 1 << (M3 - 1).bit_length()
    A_fft = np.fft.rfft(an, M3_fft)
    P_fft = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, M3_fft)
    conv_ap = np.fft.irfft(A_fft * P_fft, M3_fft)[:M3]
    h = conv_ap[2*n - 2 : n - 2 : -1]
    gradL = 2.0 * h
    grad_f = 2.0 * n * gradL
    dot = float(np.dot(an, grad_f))
    grad_x = (grad_f - an * dot) / S
    val = 2.0 * n * L
    # If nonfinite, return large
    return float(val), grad_x
```
For n=1, slice? n large. For n=2: stop n-2=0; okay. For n=1: start 0 stop -1 => index 0? But not used.

Could optimize derivative: Since p symmetric? p from c of a not symmetric. Need full.

Potential bug: `np.fft.irfft(A_fft*P_fft)` uses real FFT of an and p_rev length n/2n-1; their linear convolution. We selected h_j= conv(an, p_rev)[2n-2-j]. For n=10000 slice length 10000. Good.

But `p` length 2n-1, p_rev length same. M3=3n-2. For n=10000, M3=29998, nextpow2=32768. Good.

L-BFGS run:
```
def run_lbfgs(x0, beta, time_budget, maxiter_cap=2000, debug=False):
    n=len(x0); x=normalize_pos(x0.copy())
    # ensure max <=1000? bound 1
    bounds=[(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    # estimate
    t0=time.monotonic(); lse_norm_objective(x,beta); dt=time.monotonic()-t0
    fun_per_iter=8
    maxiter=max(100, min(maxiter_cap, int(time_budget/(dt*fun_per_iter+1e-6))))
    maxfun=max(200, int(maxiter*fun_per_iter*2))
    options={'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':10,'disp':False}
    res=minimize(lse_norm_objective, x, args=(beta,), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
    x=np.clip(res.x,0,1000.0)
    x=normalize_pos(x)
    return score_full(x), x, maxiter
```
But `bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] * n` all same tuple; okay. If x0 sum=1 but values <1, bound upper 1 not active. If L-BFGS wants scale >1? objective flat, may. Good.

Potential issue: The objective is invariant to scaling but with projection gradient, L-BFGS might see zero gradient for scale but bound at 1 may create non-smooth if some x hit 1. Not likely.

Time estimate: `dt` includes first objective maybe expensive. L-BFGS line search might call many funs; using `time_budget/(dt*fun_per_iter)` may under/over. We can set fun_per_iter=5 conservative. We'll also have outer deadline checks after each beta.

```
def lse_anneal(x0, betas, deadline):
    a=normalize_pos(x0); best=score_full(a)
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: break
       remaining=deadline - time.monotonic() - 20
       if remaining < 30: break
       sc, a_new, iters = run_lbfgs(a, beta, remaining*0.9, maxiter_cap=1500)
       if sc < best: best, a = sc, a_new
    return best,a
```

Peak shave function:
```
def peak_shave(a0, n_iters, top_k, base_step=0.1, deadline=None):
    a=normalize_pos(a0)
    best=score_full(a); best_a=a.copy()
    step=base_step
    n=len(a); lower=2.0*n/(2.0*n-1.0)
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(a); S=1; M=max(c); sc=2*n*M
       if sc<best: update
       if sc - lower < 1e-7: break
       # top k indices
       if top_k >= len(c): idx=np.arange(len(c)); weights=np.ones...
       else:
          # argpartition full c length 2n-1; okay.
          top_local=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
          vals=c[top_local]
          # weights softmax on top: exp(beta*(vals-M))
          shifted=np.clip(2000*(vals-M), -700, 0)  # M max
          w=np.exp(shifted); w/=w.sum()
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       for pos,k in enumerate(top_local):
           kk=int(k); ww=float(w[pos])
           j0=max(0,kk-n+1); j1=min(kk,n-1)
           if j1>=j0:
               # a[kk - j] for j in j0..j1 -> reversed slice a[kk-j1 : kk-j0+1 : -1]
               # Example j0=0,j1=k => a[k: -1] reversed.
               grad[j0:j1+1] += 2*ww*a[kk-j1:kk-j0+1:-1]
       # project to tangent: remove component along a? For simplex use mean?
       # We're keeping a sum1. Use g = grad - grad.mean()*ones.
       g = grad - np.mean(grad)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       # Adaptive step: maybe if previous failed reduce
       trial=step
       improved=False
       for _ in range(4):
           a_try=a - trial*g
           a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0); s=a_try.sum()
           if s>0: a_try=a_try/s
           sc_try=score_full(a_try)
           if sc_try < best: # accept new global
               best=sc_try; best_a=a_try.copy(); a=a_try; improved=True; break
           if sc_try < sc - 1e-12: # accept if improves current even if not global? But best maybe lower; using current may descend from bad? We should maintain a=best_a to avoid losing global? Better always operate from best_a, not current worsened.
       if not improved:
           step *= 0.8
       else:
           step = min(max_step, step*1.05)
```

For max objective, any step that doesn't improve worsens; subgradient descent may need accept non-improvement? Usually choose best. We can operate from current; if current worsens, reset to best. Simpler: at each iteration, start from `a_work`; if no improve, reset `a_work=best_a.copy()` and reduce step. If improve, keep. That avoids drift.

```

def spectral_flat(a0, n_iters, alpha, target_ratio=1.0):
   a=normalize(a0); best...
   M_fft for conv length
   for it:
       c=fast_conv(a); M=max(c); sc=...
       if sc<best update
       # Desired c target: maybe min(c, M*target_ratio) with small fraction of excess
       c_t = c.copy()
       thr = M * target_ratio  # start 0.98
       excess = c_t > thr
       c_t[excess] = thr + alpha*(c_t[excess]-thr)  # partially shave
       # adjust sum to 1 (since a sum1)
       c_t = c_t / c_t.sum()
       C = np.fft.rfft(c_t, M_fft)  # length M_fft (c length M, zero padded)
       A = np.fft.rfft(a, M_fft)
       ratio = np.sqrt(np.abs(C)/np.maximum(np.abs(C_old?),?))
```

def spectral_shave(a0, n_iters, alpha=0.5, target_ratio=0.98):
  a=normalize(a0); best...
  M=2n-1; M_fft=nextpow2(M)
  base=M? 
  for it:
    c=fast_conv(a); Mv=max(c); sc=...
    c_t=c.copy()
    thr=Mv*target_ratio*(1 - 0.1*it/n_iters?) maybe
    c_t[c_t>thr]=thr
    if it>0: c_t[c_t>thr]=thr + 0.1*(c_t[c_t>thr]-thr)
    c_t /= c_t.sum()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M_fft)
    C_old=A*A
    C_des=np.fft.rfft(c_t,M_fft)
    abs_old=np.abs(C_old); abs_old[abs_old<1e-300]=1e-300
    ratio=np.sqrt(np.abs(C_des)/abs_old)
    A_new=A*(1.0-alpha+alpha*ratio)
    a_new=np.fft.irfft(A_new,M_fft)[:n]
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0); normalize
    sc_new=score_full(a_new)
    if sc_new<best update; maybe if sc_new<sc keep else a=a? We can accept only if improves sc_work; else revert.
```

def spectral_shave(a0, n_iters=200, alpha=0.5, target_start=0.98, deadline=None):
    a=normalize_pos(a0); best=score_full(a); best_a=a; n=len(a); M_fft=...
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(a); Mv=max(c)
       sc=2*n*Mv
       if sc<best: update
       progress=it/max(1,n_iters)
       thr = Mv * (target_start - 0.03*progress) # from .98 to .95
       c_t = c.copy()
       c_t[c_t > thr] = thr + 0.2*(c_t[c_t>thr]-thr)
       c_t[:] /sum
       A=...
       ...
       a_new...
       sc_new=score_full(a_new)
       if sc_new < sc: a=a_new  # operate from improved
       else: a=best_a? maybe reset to best if no improve
       if sc_new < best: update
       if no improve many times break? 
```

Peak shave details:
```
def peak_shave(a0, n_iters=1000, top_k=5, base_step=0.003, max_step=0.015, deadline=None, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    best = score_full(a); best_a=a.copy()
    step = base_step
    fail=0
    n=len(a); lower=2*n/(2*n-1)
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(a); Mv=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*Mv
       if sc < best - 1e-15:
           best=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       if sc - lower < 1e-8: break
       # top indices
       if top_k >= len(c):
          idx=np.arange(len(c)); w=np.ones(len(idx))/len(idx)
       else:
          # for speed, use argpartition; ensure includes max
          part = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
          vals=c[part]
          shifted = np.clip(5000*(vals-Mv), -700, 5000)
          w=np.exp(shifted); wsum=w.sum(); if wsum<=0: w=np.ones(top_k)/top_k else w/=wsum
       grad=n.zeros(n)
       for kk,wk in zip(part,w):
          j0=max(0,int(kk)-n+1); j1=min(int(kk),n-1)
          if j1>=j0:
              grad[j0:j1+1] += 2*wk*a[j0:j1+1][::-1]
       # tangent to sum=1. Since we normalize after, use mean subtraction.
       g = grad - np.mean(grad)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm<1e-15:
           # try perturb
           break
       d = -g/gnorm
       accepted=False
       for trial in (step, step*0.5, step*0.25):
           a_try = a + trial*d
           a_try = np.maximum(a_try,0.0)
           s=a_try.sum()
           if s <=0: continue
           a_try = a_try/s
           sc_try=score_full(a_try)
           if sc_try < sc - 1e-13:
               a=a_try; accepted=True; fail=0; step=min(max_step, step*1.05)
               if sc_try < best: best=sc_try; best_a=a_try.copy()
               break
       if not accepted:
           fail+=1; step=max(1e-5, step*0.7)
           if fail > 30:
               a = best_a.copy()
               fail = 0
    return best, best_a
```
This uses one `score_full` per trial (fft). top_k maybe 5-20; direct gradient loops top_k * slice length ~ top_k*n? For n=10000, top_k=20, copying 20*10000=200k, fine. 1000 iters=200M copy maybe slow? Could be. Use direct for top_k=5 to reduce. For higher top_k, okay.

`score_full` after line search recomputes conv, so each iter at least one extra. 1000 iters maybe okay. We can reduce n_iters for high top_k.

```
sc, a = run_lbfgs(best_a, 5000, time_budget=300)
```
Then peak again.

def expand_candidate(a, new_n, budget):

   sc=score_full(big)

Implementation details:
`normalize_pos`:
```
def normalize_pos(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a.fill(0.0)
        if a.size>0: a[0]=1.0
        return a
    return a/s
```
If input length odd maybe.

`make_binomial`:
```
def make_binomial(n, power=1.0):
    b=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    b[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
        b[i]=b[i-1]*((2*i-1)/(2*i))
    if power != 1.0:
       if power == 0: return normalize_pos(np.ones(n))
       b=np.power(np.maximum(b,1e-12), power)
    return normalize_pos(b)
```
Loop 10000 Python okay. Could vectorize cumulative product: factors = (2*np.arange(1,n)-1)/(2*np.arange(1,n)); b[1:]=np.cumprod(factors); faster. Use that.
```
if n>1:
    i=np.arange(1,n)
    b[1:]=np.cumprod((2*i-1)/(2*i))
```
Good.

`make_power_law`: `b=(np.arange(n)+1.0)**(-p)`, normalize.
`make_geometric`: `b=np.power(r, np.arange(n))`; normalize.
`make_taper`: `x=np.linspace(0,1,n); b=a*(1+t*x)`; if min <=0, b=np.maximum(b,1e-12). Return normalize.
`make_quad`: `b=a*(1+q*(x-0.5)**2)`.
`make_sin`: `b=a*(1+amp*np.cos(2π freq x + phase))`; max positive.
`make_random_perturb`: as above.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
  t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-30
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  parent=None; try load...
  n_main=10000
  if parent is not None and 0 < len(parent) < n_main/2: maybe n_main=max(64,min(len(parent),12000))? But use 10000.
  seeds=seed_sweep(n_main,parent,rng)
  if not seeds: baseline binomial
  best_score, best_a = seeds[0]
  print top
  # Spectral
  if time.monotonic() < deadline-300:
      sc,a = spectral_shave(best_a, n_iters=300, alpha=0.5, target_start=0.98, deadline=min(deadline-250, t0+120))
      if sc<best_score: update
  # LSE anneal
  if time.monotonic() < deadline-200:
      a_work=best_a.copy()
      # if parent much lower, maybe betas skip low
      betas=[5,20,80,300,1000,3000]
      for beta in betas:
          current_time=time.monotonic()
          remaining=deadline - current_time - 150  # reserve final
          if remaining < 60: break
          time_budget=min(240, remaining*0.4)
          sc, a_new, iters = run_lbfgs(a_work, beta, time_budget, maxiter_cap=1200)
          print
          if sc < best_score: update best
          # decide a_work
          if sc < best_score + 0.005:
              a_work=a_new
          else:
              a_work=best_a.copy()
  # Peak shave passes
  for top_k, it_per, label in [(3,1500),(8,1000),(20,700)]:
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()-120
       if remaining < 30: break
       n_iters=min(it_per, int(remaining*30)) # cap
       sc,a=peak_shave(best_a, n_iters, top_k, base_step=0.004, max_step=0.02, deadline=min(deadline-100, t0+?))
       if sc<best_score update
  # LSE high after peak
  for beta in [2000,8000]:
       remaining... time_budget=120
       sc,a=run_lbfgs(best_a,beta,...)
  # peak again top 3,5
  # multi-start perturb
  seeds2 = []
  for i in range(5):
      if time left >300:
         if i%2==0: a_try=perturb_mult(best_a,rng,0.02+0.01*i)
         else: a_try=make_mixed(best_a, make_binomial(n_main), 0.1+0.05*i)
         sc_try=score_full(a_try)
         # quick LSE beta 1000 time 60
         sc,a=run_lbfgs(a_try,1000,time_budget=60,maxiter_cap=400)
         if sc<best update
         # quick peak
  # length expansion
  if time left >200:
      for new_n in [n_main+2000, n_main*2//? maybe 16000]:
         if new_n > 20000: continue
         big=interpolate_seq(best_a,new_n)
         sc=score_full(big)
         if sc < best_score*0.999? maybe update if lower
         if sc < best_score or sc < best_score+0.002:
             sc2,a2=peak_shave(big, 400, top_k=5,time_budget... )
             if sc2<best update
         # Also binomial new_n quick?
  # Final check
  if len(best_a) <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < deadline-20:
      out_temp=[float(max(0,min(1000,x))) for x in best_a]
      ver=evaluate_sequence(out_temp)
      if np.isfinite(ver):
          print internal best_score vs ver
          # If ver differs significantly? choose? We only have one candidate. We can't optimize for ver. But if ver < internal? update best_score? It changes metric? If ver is actual, use it. If ver is bigger due numeric, no.
      # Should we select between best_a and uniform if ver? Not needed.
  out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
  if sum(out) < 0.01: out=[0.5]
  return out
```

Let's verify gradient formula with FFT selection one more time. Q=∑_k p_k c_k, c=conv(a,a). ∂Q/∂a_j = 2∑_{i:0≤i,k-i<n} p_{i+j} a_i (because each term p_{i+j} a_i a_j; also if i and k-i swap? Let's derive using Q = ∑_{i,j} a_i a_j p_{i+j}. For a fixed j, terms with index j as first: ∑_i a_i p_{i+j} a_j => a_j * ∑_i p_{i+j} a_i. Terms with j as second: ∑_i a_j a_i p_{i+j} => same. So total 2 a_j ∑_i p_{i+j}a_i. But if i=j and p_{2j} term, double counting includes both first and second contributions to a_j^2 p_{2j}, derivative 2a_j p_{2j}. Formula 2 a_j p_{2j} a_j? Wait the term in Q matrix includes diagonal a_j^2 p_{2j}. In sum over i,j, (i,j) and (j,i) are same when i=j; the double-sum actually counts diagonal once? Q=∑_{i,j} a_i a_j p_{i+j}; for i=j, just a_i^2 p_{2i}. If we write gradient as 2(Ha)_j with H_{ij}=p_{i+j}, then for diagonal, 2H_{jj}a_j=2p_{2j}a_j correct. For off-diagonal, H_{ij} term a_i a_j appears twice in sum i,j; derivative 2p_{i+j}a_j? Formula 2∑_i p_{i+j} a_i gives for fixed j sum over i includes i and j' counterpart? For fixed k? Let's test single p_k. Q=c_k. H_{ij}=1 if i+j=k. Q=∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j. For offdiag pairs, sum includes both (i,j) and (j,i) if we sum all i,j; but c_k as normally written ∑_i a_i a_{k-i} includes each unordered pair twice? For k=1, i=0,1: c1=a0a1+a1a0=2a0a1. Yes standard cpoly coefficient includes both. Then derivative wrt a0 = a1 + a1 =2a1. Formula 2 a0? ∑_i p_{i+j}a_i for j=0 p_k? p index k: ∑_i a_i if i=k? p_{i}=1 when i=k => a_k, times 2 =>2a1 good. For j=1 =>2a0. Good. If k even and j=k/2, formula gives 2a_j. derivative of a_j^2 is 2a_j. Good. So derivative =2 ∑_i p_{i+j} a_i. Where p_{i+j} nonzero only if p active. This matches direct: for active k, h_j=2a_{k-j}. Good.
FFT: We need h/2 = ∑_i p_{i+j}a_i. We found conv(a,p_rev)[2n-2-j]. Let's prove with indices: conv(a,p_rev)_m=∑_i a_i p_rev_{m-i}=∑_i a_i p_{L-1-(m-i)}. Set m=2n-2-j = L-1-j. Then p_{L-1 - (L-1-j - i)}=p_{j+i}. Good. Sum over valid i. Perfect.

Now, in direct gradient for weighted p, we used `grad[j0:j1+1] += 2*wk*a[j0:j1+1][::-1]`. Here j0=max(0,k-n+1), j1=min(k,n-1). For each j, a_{k-j} is from a[j1-j0] reversed. Good.

Implement `lse_log_objective(z, beta)`:
```
def lse_log_objective(z,beta):
   z=np.asarray(z)
   zm=z.max()
   if not finite: return inf, zeros
   e=np.exp(z-zm)
   s=e/e.sum()
   # s positive, sum1
   c=fast_conv(s); ... grad_f wrt s
   dot = np.dot(s, grad_f)
   grad_z = s*(grad_f - dot)
   val=2nL
```
This is elegant. Bounds no. However if L-BFGS moves z extremely, e underflow some s zeros; gradient zeros for those, no recovery. But likely not. We can clip z range inside objective? If z range > 50, set e for min = exp(-50) to allow? Could add `z_shift = np.clip(z - zm, -50, 0)` to keep smallest s ~1e-22, enough. This creates flat clipping but helps. Use `-50` to avoid underflow and keep positive. But derivative at clip not exact; if L-BFGS tries to drive to zero, it will stop. Fine.

def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, time_budget, maxiter_cap=1500):
    a=normalize_pos(a0)
    z0=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z0-=np.mean(z0)
    # time estimate objective
    t0=time.monotonic(); val,grad = lse_log_objective(z0,beta); dt=...
    maxfun = int(max(300, min(20000, time_budget/max(dt*2,1e-5))))
    maxiter = int(max(100, min(maxiter_cap, maxfun//8)))
    options={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-9,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':10}
    res=minimize(lse_log_objective, z0, args=(beta,), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options=options)
    # convert
    z=res.x
    zm=z.max(); e=np.exp(np.clip(z-zm,-50,0)); s=e/e.sum(); s=normalize_pos(s)
    sc=score_full(s)
    if not finite: fallback a0
```

```
def softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    zm = np.max(z)
    if not np.isfinite(zm): return np.full(n,1/n)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z-zm, -50, 0))
    s = e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full(np.size(z), 1.0/np.size(z))
    return e/s
```

```
def lse_log_objective(z,beta):
    z=np.asarray(z)
    n=z.size
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e300, np.zeros(n)
    zm=z.max()
    if not np.isfinite(zm): ...
    zc=np.clip(z-zm, -50, 0)
    e=np.exp(zc)
    se=e.sum()
    if se <=0: return 1e300, zeros
    s=e/se
    c=fast_conv(s)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    shifted=np.clip(beta*(c-cmax), -700, 700)
    exps=np.exp(shifted)
    zexp=exps.sum()
    if zexp<=0 or not finite: return 1e300, zeros
    L=cmax + np.log(zexp)/beta
    p=exps/zexp
    # derivative of L wrt s
    use_direct = False
    nonz=np.nonzero(p>1e-10)[0]
    if nonz.size <= 128:
        h=np.zeros(n)
        for kk in nonz:
            k=int(kk); w=float(p[k])
            j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
            if j1>=j0:
                h[j0:j1+1] += 2.0*w*s[j0:j1+1][::-1]
    else:
        p_rev=p[::-1]
        M3=3*n-2
        M3_fft=1 << (M3 - 1).bit_length()
        A=np.fft.rfft(s,M3_fft); P=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,M3_fft)
        conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,M3_fft)[:M3]
        h=conv[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]
        h *= 2.0
    grad_f = 2.0*n*h
    dot=float(np.dot(s, grad_f))
    grad_z = s*(grad_f - dot)
    val = 2.0*n*L
    if not finite val: return 1e300, zeros
    grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)
    return float(val), grad_z
```
For n maybe 1? slice issue. If n<2, handle separately? Not needed but robust:

if n==1: c=[s0^2=1], L=1, grad_f=2, dot=2, grad_z=0.
```

`spectral_shave`: Use `score_full(a)` each accepted? We already compute c. For a_new, count fails. Important: If accepting only when sc_new < sc (current), and current starting from best, we may miss steps that lower LSE but raise max? We want max. Good.
```
def spectral_shave(a0,n_iters=200,alpha=0.5,target_start=0.98,target_end=0.95,deadline=None):
    a=normalize_pos(a0); best=score_full(a); best_a=a.copy()
    n=len(a); M=2*n-1; M_fft=1<<(M-1).bit_length()
    fails=0
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(a); c=np.maximum(c,0); Mv=max(c); sc=2*n*Mv
       if sc<best: update
       prog=it/max(1,n_iters)
       thr=Mv*(target_start + (target_end-target_start)*prog)
       c_t=c.copy()
       over=c_t>thr
       if np.any(over):
          c_t[over]=thr + 0.25*(c_t[over]-thr)
       csum=c_t.sum()
       if csum>0: c_t/=csum
       if np.all(c_t==c): break? no
       C_des=np.fft.rfft(c_t,M_fft)
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,M_fft)
       C_old=A*A
       ratio=np.sqrt(np.abs(C_des)/np.maximum(np.abs(C_old),1e-30))
       ratio=np.clip(ratio,0.0,5.0)
       A_new=A*((1.0-alpha)+alpha*ratio)
       a_new=np.fft.irfft(A_new,M_fft)[:n]
       a_new=normalize_pos(a_new)
       sc_new=score_full(a_new)
       if sc_new < sc - 1e-12:
          a=a_new; fails=0; if sc_new<best update
       else:
          fails+=1
          if fails>20:
             a=best_a.copy(); fails=0
             # lower alpha? cannot modify local? can set alpha=alpha*0.8
             alpha=max(0.1,alpha*0.8)
    return best,best_a
```
Need `alpha` local mutable. Good.

    if rng is None: rng = np.random.default_rng(20240518)
    out=[]

    for q in (-0.5,0.2,0.5): out append make_quad_mod(base,q)

        else:
           a=interpolate_seq(parent,n); out...
           # also maybe if parent length close? no
    # remove duplicates? not needed
    out.sort(key=seed_score_key)
    # keep top maybe 10 but return list

def run_lbfgs(a0,beta,time_budget,maxiter_cap=1500):

def softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    n=z.size
    if n==0: return z
    zm=np.max(z)
    if not np.isfinite(zm): return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z-zm,-50,0))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return e/s
```

Let's produce final.```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2).

The implementation combines a mathematically motivated initial family
(leading coefficients of sqrt(1 + z + ... + z^(2n-2))), fast FFT-based
exact scoring, spectral peak shaving, log-space L-BFGS on a log-sum-exp
smoothed objective, exact top-lag subgradient peak shaving, and a few
multi-start / length-expansion polishing phases.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def normalize_pos(a):
    """Clip to non-negative and normalize sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.zeros_like(a)
        a[0] = 1.0
        return a
    return a / s


def softmax_pos(z):
    """Stable softmax for log-space variables, clipped to avoid underflow."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z - zm, -60.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return e / s


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution a * a by FFT, length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    m_fft = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m_fft)[:m]
    return c


def score_full(a):
    """Exact objective 2n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2 for non-negative a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    an = a / s
    c = fast_conv(an)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    val = 2.0 * n * mx / (s * s)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def make_binomial(n, power=1.0):
    """
    Coefficients C(2k,k)/4^k, which are the first coefficients of
    sqrt(1/(1-z)).  This is a strong near-optimal non-negative seed.
    """
    b = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        i = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64)
        b[1:] = np.cumprod((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))
    if power != 1.0:
        if power == 0.0:
            return normalize_pos(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        b = np.power(np.maximum(b, 1e-12), float(power))
    return normalize_pos(b)


def make_power_law(n, p):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    b = np.power(i, -float(p))
    return normalize_pos(b)


def make_geometric(n, r):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.power(float(r), x)
    return normalize_pos(b)


def make_mixed(a, b, alpha):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.shape != b.shape:
        b = interpolate_seq(b, a.size)
    return normalize_pos((1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * b)


def make_taper_linear(a, t):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = a * (1.0 + float(t) * x)
    return normalize_pos(np.maximum(b, 1e-12))


def make_quad_mod(a, q):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = a * (1.0 + float(q) * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
    return normalize_pos(np.maximum(b, 1e-12))


def make_sin_mod(a, amp, freq, phase):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = a * (1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x + float(phase)))
    return normalize_pos(np.maximum(b, 1e-12))


def make_random_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.10, max_modes=6):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    pert = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, int(max_modes) + 1):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 13))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(strength) / float(m)
        pert += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    b = a * (1.0 + pert)
    return normalize_pos(np.maximum(b, 1e-12))


def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
    """Interpolate a sequence to a new length (linear) and normalize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == new_n:
        return normalize_pos(a)
    if n == 0:
        return normalize_pos(np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64))
    if n == 1:
        return decode _seq?  # placeholder
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(new_n), dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_pos(np.interp(x_new, x_old, a))


def seed_score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def seed_sweep(n, parent=None, rng=None):
    """Fast evaluation of deterministic and random seeds."""
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240518)

    out = []
    base = make_binomial(n, 1.0)
    s = score_full(base)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        out.append((s, base.copy()))

    # Non-negativity-preserving intensities of the binomial seed.
    for pw in (0.25, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.80, 1.20):
        a = make_binomial(n, pw)
        s = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            out.append((s, a.copy()))

    uni = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    for al in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
        a = make_mixed(base, uni, al)
        s = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            out.append((s, a.copy()))

    # Asymmetric tapers / quadratic shapes (orientation matters for A(z)^2).
    for t in (-0.50, -0.20, 0.20, 0.50, 0.80):
        a = make_taper_linear(base, t)
        s = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            out.append((s, a.copy()))

    for q in (-0.50, 0.20, 0.50):
        a = make_quad_mod(base, q)
        s = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            out.append((s, a.copy()))

    # Low-frequency sinusoidal modulations.
    for f in (1, 3, 7):
        for amp in (0.10, 0.20):
            for ph in (0.0, np.pi):
                a = make_sin_mod(base, amp, f, ph)
                sc = score_full(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    # Power/geometric tails.
    for r in (0.90, 0.95, 0.98, 0.995):
        a = make_geometric(n, r)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))
    for p in (0.20, 0.30, 0.60, 0.80):
        a = make_power_law(n, p)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    # Smooth random perturbations of the best base.
    for k in range(6):
        a = make_random_perturb(base, rng, strength=0.10 + 0.05 * k, max_modes=6)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    if parent is not None:
        parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent.size > 10:
            if parent.size == n:
                a = normalize_pos(np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0))
            else:
                a = interpolate_seq(np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0), n)
            sc = score_full(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    out.sort(key=seed_score_key)
    return out


def _lse_derivative_smoothing(a, p, beta):
    """
    Derivative of LSE_beta(conv(a,a)) with respect to a.
    Uses direct construction for sparse p, FFT otherwise.
    """
    n = a.size
    nonz = np.nonzero(p > 1e-10)[0]
    if nonz.size <= 16:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in nonz:
            k = int(kk)
            w = float(p[kk])
            if w == 0.0:
                continue
            j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j1 = min(k, n - 1)
            if j1 >= j0:
                h[j0:j1 + 1] += 2.0 * w * a[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]
        return h

    p_rev = p[::-1]
    m3 = 3 * n - 2
    if m3 <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m3_fft = 1 << (m3 - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m3_fft)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, m3_fft)
    conv_ap = np.fft.irfft(A * P, m3_fft)[:m3]
    # h_j = 2 * sum_i p_{i+j} a_i, selected as conv(a, rev(p))[2n-2-j]
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0 * p[0] * a
    h = conv_ap[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]
    return 2.0 * h


def lse_log_objective(z, beta):
    """
    Scaled LSE-smoothed objective in log-space.
    Returning n*objective and n*gradient improves L-BFGS scaling.
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    e = np.exp(np.clip(z - zm, -60.0, 0.0))
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    sarr = e / se

    c = fast_conv(sarr)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    shifted = np.clip(float(beta) * (c - cmax), -700.0, 700.0)
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    zexp = float(exps.sum())
    if zexp <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(zexp):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L = cmax + float(np.log(zexp)) / float(beta)
    p = exps / zexp

    grad_L = _lse_derivative_smoothing(sarr, p, beta)
    # f(a) = 2n L(a), with a normalized.  Gradient wrt simplex coordinate a:
    grad_f = 2.0 * float(n) * grad_L
    dot = float(np.dot(sarr, grad_f))
    # Log-softmax Jacobian:
    grad_z = float(n) * sarr * (grad_f - dot)  # extra n for scaling
    val = float(n) * (2.0 * float(n) * L)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)
    return val, grad_z


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, time_budget, maxiter_cap=1800):
    """
    Time-limited L-BFGS in log-space on the LSE-smoothed objective.
    Returns (score, array, estimated_function_eval_budget).
    """
    a0 = normalize_pos(a0)
    n = a0.size
    z0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    z0 = z0 - float(np.mean(z0))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        lse_log_objective(z0, beta)
    except Exception:
        return score_full(a0), a0.copy(), 0
    dt = max(time.monotonic() - t0, 1e-6)

    maxfun = int(max(300, min(30000, max(30.0, float(time_budget)) / max(dt * 2.0, 1e-6))))
    maxiter = int(max(100, min(maxiter_cap, max(100, maxfun // 8))))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_log_objective,
            z0,
            args=(float(beta),),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxls": 10,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        z = z0

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return score_full(a0), a0.copy(), maxfun

    sarr = softmax_pos(z)
    sarr = normalize_pos(sarr)
    sc = score_full(sarr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e50:
        return score_full(a0), a0.copy(), maxfun
    return sc, sarr, maxfun


def spectral_shave(a0, n_iters=200, alpha=0.5, target_start=0.98,
                   target_end=0.94, deadline=None):
    """
    Alternate time-domain non-negativity/shaving with spectral amplitude
    update that drives flatting of A(z)^2 coefficients.
    """
    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = score_full(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    m = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    fails = 0

    for it in range(int(n_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = fast_conv(a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mval = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * mval
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best:
            best = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        prog = float(it) / max(1, int(n_iters))
        thr = mval * (float(target_start) + (float(target_end) - float(target_start)) * prog)
        ct = c.copy()
        over = ct > thr
        if np.any(over):
            ct[over] = thr + 0.25 * (ct[over] - thr)
        csum = float(ct.sum())
        if csum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(csum):
            break
        ct /= csum

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
        C_old = A * A
        C_des = np.fft.rfft(ct, m_fft)
        den = np.abs(C_old)
        den = np.where(den > 1e-30, den, 1e-30)
        ratio = np.sqrt(np.abs(C_des) / den)
        ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.0, 5.0)

        A_new = A * ((1.0 - alpha) + alpha * ratio)
        a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, m_fft)[:n]
        a_new = normalize_pos(a_new)
        sc_new = score_full(a_new)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < sc - 1e-12:
            a = a_new
            fails = 0
            if sc_new < best:
                best = sc_new
                best_a = a_new.copy()
        else:
            fails += 1
            if fails > 25:
                a = best_a.copy()
                fails = 0
                alpha = max(0.15, alpha * 0.75)

    return best, best_a


def peak_shave(a0, n_iters=1000, top_k=5, base_step=0.002, max_step=0.012,
               deadline=None):
    """
    Exact subgradient peak shaving for max coefficient of A(z)^2.
    Uses a small active set of lags and projected gradient steps on simplex.
    """
    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = score_full(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    step = float(base_step)
    max_step = float(max_step)
    lower = 2.0 * float(n) / (2.0 * float(n) - 1.0)
    fails = 0
    lag_len = 2 * n - 1

    for _ in range(int(n_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = fast_conv(a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mval = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * mval
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best:
            best = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if sc - lower < 1e-10:
            break

        tk = int(min(top_k, lag_len))
        if tk >= lag_len:
            idx = np.arange(lag_len, dtype=np.int64)
            vals = c[idx]
            weights = np.ones(tk, dtype=np.float64) / float(tk)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -tk)[-tk:]
            vals = c[idx]
            shifted = np.clip(2000.0 * (vals - mval), -700.0, 0.0)
            weights = np.exp(shifted)
            wsum = float(weights.sum())
            if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
                weights = np.ones(tk, dtype=np.float64) / float(tk)
            else:
                weights /= wsum

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for pos, kk in enumerate(idx):
            k = int(kk)
            w = float(weights[pos])
            if w == 0.0:
                continue
            j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j1 = min(k, n - 1)
            if j1 >= j0:
                grad[j0:j1 + 1] += 2.0 * w * a[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]

        # Tangential direction for sum(a)=1.
        g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break
        d = -g / gnorm

        accepted = False
        for trial in (step, step * 0.5, step * 0.25, step * 0.1):
            a_try = a + trial * d
            a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 0.0)
            s_try = float(a_try.sum())
            if s_try <= 1e-300:
                continue
            a_try = a_try / s_try
            sc_try = score_full(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < sc - 1e-13:
                a = a_try
                accepted = True
                fails = 0
                step = min(max_step, step * 1.07)
                if sc_try < best:
                    best = sc_try
                    best_a = a.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            step = max(1e-5, step * 0.72)
            fails += 1
            if fails > 45:
                a = best_a.copy()
                fails = 0

    return best, best_a


def random_coord_polish(a0, max_trials=300, rng=None, deadline=None):
    """Small random multiplicative perturbations on heavy coordinates."""
    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = score_full(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)

    for it in range(int(max_trials)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        if it % 40 == 0:
            top = np.argpartition(a, -min(500, n))[-min(500, n):]
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(a, -min(500, n))[-min(500, n):]
        idx = int(top[int(rng.integers(top.size))])
        f = float(np.exp(rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35)))
        a_try = a.copy()
        a_try[idx] *= f
        a_try = normalize_pos(a_try)
        sc = score_full(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best:
            best = sc
            best_a = a_try.copy()

    return best, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Entry point.  Uses the global height_sequence_1 if present as a seed,
    otherwise starts from strong binomial-based constructions.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = min(float(budget_s), 2400.0)
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, time_limit - 60.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n_main = 10000

    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size >= 32:
                parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        # Allow a parent at another length to seed the main length, but remember it.
        pass

    print(f"Starting search, budget={time_limit:.0f}s, n_main={n_main}")

    # Initial deterministic/random seed sweep.
    seeds = seed_sweep(n_main, parent, rng)
    if not seeds:
        best_score = score_full(make_binomial(n_main, 1.0))
        best_a = make_binomial(n_main, 1.0)
    else:
        best_score, best_a = seeds[0]
        best_a = normalize_pos(best_a.copy())
        best_score = score_full(best_a)

    print(f"Initial best: {best_score:.8f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # Optional spectral pre-shaping.  Shorter if a very good parent is present.
    spectral_budget = 90.0 if best_score > 1.53 else 35.0
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - max(120.0, spectral_budget):
        spec_deadline = min(deadline - 240.0, time.monotonic() + spectral_budget)
        sc, a = spectral_shave(best_a, n_iters=500, alpha=0.55,
                               target_start=0.98, target_end=0.94,
                               deadline=spec_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            print(f"Spectral shave: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Main log-LSE annealing.
    if best_score > 1.53:
        betas = [10.0, 40.0, 150.0, 600.0, 2000.0, 8000.0]
    else:
        betas = [40.0, 150.0, 600.0, 2000.0, 8000.0]

    a_work = best_a.copy()
    for beta in betas:
        now = time.monotonic()
        reserve = 300.0  # keep time for peak shaving / final / verification
        remaining = deadline - now - reserve
        if remaining < 45.0:
            break
        tb = min(180.0, 0.38 * max(30.0, remaining))
        sc, a2, nf = run_lbfgs(a_work, beta, tb, maxiter_cap=1800)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            print(f"LSE beta={beta:7.0f}: internal={sc:.8f} (n={a2.size}, fun~{nf})")
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a2.copy()
            # Continue from latest if it remains competitive; otherwise from best.
            if sc < best_score + 0.004:
                a_work = a2.copy()
            else:
                a_work = best_a.copy()
        else:
            a_work = best_a.copy()

    # Exact subgradient peak shaving in increasing active-set width.
    for tk, cap_iters, reserve in ((3, 2200, 360), (10, 1500, 250), (25, 900, 180)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = deadline - now - reserve
        if remaining < 25.0:
            break
        # Conservative adaptive iteration budget.  Each iteration may call score_full
        # once or a few times; 40 complete/s is a safe cap at n~10000.
        n_iters = max(300, min(cap_iters, int(remaining * 40.0 * (5.0 / float(tk + 2.0)))))
        phase_deadline = min(deadline - (reserve - 5.0), time.monotonic() + remaining)
        sc, a2 = peak_shave(best_a, n_iters=n_iters, top_k=tk,
                            base_step=0.0018, max_step=0.010,
                            deadline=phase_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print(f"Peak shave top_k={tk}: {best_score:.8f}")

    # After exact peak shaving, a high-beta smooth LSE can re-flatten nearby lags.
    for beta in (3000.0, 12000.0):
        now = time.monotonic()
        reserve = 180.0
        remaining = deadline - now - reserve
        if remaining < 35.0:
            break
        tb = min(110.0, 0.45 * max(25.0, remaining))
        sc, a2, nf = run_lbfgs(best_a, beta, tb, maxiter_cap=1200)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print(f"Post-peak LSE beta={beta:.0f}: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Final exact shave / random coordinate polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0
        if remaining > 30.0:
            sc, a2 = peak_shave(best_a, n_iters=int(min(1500, remaining * 35.0)),
                                top_k=5, base_step=0.0012, max_step=0.006,
                                deadline=deadline - 60.0)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a2.copy()
                print(f"Final peak shave: {best_score:.8f}")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
        sc, a2 = random_coord_polish(best_a, max_trials=int(min(800, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 35.0) * 120.0)),
                                     rng=rng, deadline=deadline - 35.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print(f"Random coordinate polish: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Controlled length expansion: try 12000-14000 if time remains.  This often
    # gives a small benefit, but final sequence is kept modest for safety.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        current_n = best_a.size
        for new_n in (12000, 14000):
            if new_n <= current_n or new_n > 16000:
                continue
            now = time.monotonic()
            remaining = deadline - now - 90.0
            if remaining < 45.0:
                break
            big = interpolate_seq(best_a, new_n)
            sc_big = score_full(big)
            print(f"Interpolated to n={new_n}: {sc_big:.8f}")
            if sc_big < best_score - 1e-6 or sc_big < best_score + 0.0005:
                # Short polish on the larger sequence.
                big_deadline = min(deadline - 80.0, now + min(90.0, remaining * 0.45))
                sc_big2, a_big = peak_shave(big, n_iters=int(min(800, (big_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 35.0)),
                                            top_k=5, base_step=0.0012, max_step=0.006,
                                            deadline=big_deadline)
                if np.isfinite(sc_big2) and sc_big2 < sc_big:
                    sc_big = sc_big2
                    big = a_big.copy()
                if sc_big < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_big
                    best_a = big.copy()
                    print(f"Length expansion improved: {best_score:.8f} at n={best_a.size}")
            # Also try a fresh binomial seed at the larger length if better than current.
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0 and new_n == 12000:
                bnew = make_binomial(new_n, 1.0)
                sc_b = score_full(bnew)
                if sc_b < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_b
                    best_a = bnew.copy()

    # Final safety: if best sequence is somehow invalid, use binomial.
    if best_a is None or best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = make_binomial(10000, 1.0)
        best_score = score_full(best_a)

    best_a = normalize_pos(np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0))

    # Optional direct verification using the provided evaluator.  The internal
    # FFT score is exact for the stated evaluator, but this guards against
    # any environment-specific surprises.
    try:
        if "evaluate_sequence" in globals() and best_a.size <= 14000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            tentative = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
            if sum(tentative) > 0.01:
                ver = evaluate_sequence(tentative)
                if np.isfinite(ver):
                    print(f"Verification: internal={best_score:.8f}, external={ver:.8f}, n={len(tentative)}")
                    # Do not change the candidate; external score is only a sanity check.
                else:
                    print("Verification returned non-finite; using fallback.")
    except Exception:
        pass

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if not out or sum(out) <= 0.01:
        out = [0.5, 0.5]

    final_time = time.monotonic() - t0
    print(f"FINAL internal score={best_score:.8f}, length={len(out)}, time={final_time:.1f}s")
    return out
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2).

The implementation combines:
  - a mathematically motivated initial family based on coefficients of
    sqrt(1/(1-z)), which are near-optimal for flattening A(z)^2,
  - exact FFT-based scoring of linear convolution,
  - spectral peak-shaving iterations,
  - log-space L-BFGS on a log-sum-exp smoothed objective,
  - exact top-lag subgradient peak shaving,
  - light multi-start and controlled length expansion.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def normalize_pos(a):
    """Clip to non-negative and normalize sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.zeros_like(a)
        a[0] = 1.0
        return a
    return a / s


def softmax_pos(z):
    """Stable softmax for log-space variables."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z - zm, -60.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return e / s


def fast_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution a * a via FFT, length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m_fft)[:m]
    return c


def score_full(a):
    """Exact objective: 2n * max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    an = a / s
    c = fast_conv(an)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    val = 2.0 * n * mx / (s * s)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def make_binomial(n, power=1.0):
    """
    Coefficients C(2k,k)/4^k, the first coefficients of sqrt(1/(1-z)).
    This is a strong near-optimal non-negative starting shape.
    """
    b = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        i = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64)
        b[1:] = np.cumprod((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))
    if power != 1.0:
        if power == 0.0:
            return normalize_pos(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        b = np.power(np.maximum(b, 1e-12), float(power))
    return normalize_pos(b)


def make_power_law(n, p):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    b = np.power(i, -float(p))
    return normalize_pos(b)


def make_geometric(n, r):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.power(float(r), x)
    return normalize_pos(b)


def make_mixed(a, b, alpha):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.shape != b.shape:
        b = interpolate_seq(b, a.size)
    return normalize_pos((1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * b)


def make_taper_linear(a, t):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = a * (1.0 + float(t) * x)
    return normalize_pos(np.maximum(b, 1e-12))


def make_quad_mod(a, q):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = a * (1.0 + float(q) * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
    return normalize_pos(np.maximum(b, 1e-12))


def make_sin_mod(a, amp, freq, phase):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = a * (1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x + float(phase)))
    return normalize_pos(np.maximum(b, 1e-12))


def make_random_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.10, max_modes=6):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    pert = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, int(max_modes) + 1):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 13))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(strength) / float(m)
        pert += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    b = a * (1.0 + pert)
    return normalize_pos(np.maximum(b, 1e-12))


def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
    """Interpolate a sequence to a new length and normalize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if n == new_n:
        return normalize_pos(a)
    if n == 0:
        return normalize_pos(np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64))
    if n == 1:
        return normalize_pos(np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64))
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_pos(np.interp(x_new, x_old, a))


def seed_score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def seed_sweep(n, parent=None, rng=None):
    """Fast evaluation of a broad deterministic/random seed pool."""
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240518)

    out = []
    base = make_binomial(n, 1.0)
    s = score_full(base)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        out.append((s, base.copy()))

    for pw in (0.25, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.80, 1.20):
        a = make_binomial(n, pw)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    uni = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    for al in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
        a = make_mixed(base, uni, al)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    for t in (-0.50, -0.20, 0.20, 0.50, 0.80):
        a = make_taper_linear(base, t)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    for q in (-0.50, 0.20, 0.50):
        a = make_quad_mod(base, q)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    for f in (1, 3, 7):
        for amp in (0.10, 0.20):
            for ph in (0.0, np.pi):
                a = make_sin_mod(base, amp, f, ph)
                sc = score_full(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    for r in (0.90, 0.95, 0.98, 0.995):
        a = make_geometric(n, r)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    for p in (0.20, 0.30, 0.60, 0.80):
        a = make_power_law(n, p)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    for k in range(6):
        a = make_random_perturb(base, rng, strength=0.10 + 0.05 * k, max_modes=6)
        sc = score_full(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    if parent is not None:
        parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent.size > 10:
            if parent.size == n:
                a = normalize_pos(np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0))
            else:
                a = interpolate_seq(np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0), n)
            sc = score_full(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                out.append((sc, a.copy()))

    out.sort(key=seed_score_key)
    return out


def lse_derivative(a, p):
    """
    Derivative of LSE_beta(conv(a,a)) with respect to a.
    Uses direct lag accumulation for very sparse p, FFT otherwise.
    """
    n = a.size
    nonz = np.nonzero(p > 1e-10)[0]

    if nonz.size <= 16:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in nonz:
            k = int(kk)
            w = float(p[kk])
            if w == 0.0:
                continue
            j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j1 = min(k, n - 1)
            if j1 >= j0:
                h[j0:j1 + 1] += 2.0 * w * a[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]
        return h

    p_rev = p[::-1]
    m3 = 3 * n - 2
    if m3 <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m3_fft = 1 << (m3 - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m3_fft)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, m3_fft)
    conv_ap = np.fft.irfft(A * P, m3_fft)[:m3]

    if n == 1:
        return 2.0 * p[0] * a
    h = conv_ap[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]
    return 2.0 * h


def lse_log_objective(z, beta):
    """
    Scaled LSE-smoothed objective in log-space.
    The n-scale on objective/gradient improves L-BFGS numeric scaling.
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    zm = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zm):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    e = np.exp(np.clip(z - zm, -60.0, 0.0))
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    sarr = e / se
    c = fast_conv(sarr)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    shifted = np.clip(float(beta) * (c - cmax), -700.0, 700.0)
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    zexp = float(exps.sum())
    if zexp <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(zexp):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L = cmax + float(np.log(zexp)) / float(beta)
    p = exps / zexp

    grad_L = lse_derivative(sarr, p)
    grad_f = 2.0 * float(n) * grad_L
    dot = float(np.dot(sarr, grad_f))

    # Log-softmax Jacobian times gradient; extra n for scaling.
    grad_z = float(n) * sarr * (grad_f - dot)
    val = float(n) * (2.0 * float(n) * L)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)
    return val, grad_z


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, time_budget, maxiter_cap=1800):
    """
    Time-limited L-BFGS in log-space on the LSE-smoothed objective.
    Returns (score, array, estimated_function_budget).
    """
    a0 = normalize_pos(a0)
    z0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    z0 = z0 - float(np.mean(z0))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        lse_log_objective(z0, beta)
    except Exception:
        return score_full(a0), a0.copy(), 0
    dt = max(time.monotonic() - t0, 1e-6)

    maxfun = int(max(300, min(30000, max(30.0, float(time_budget)) / max(dt * 2.0, 1e-6))))
    maxiter = int(max(100, min(maxiter_cap, max(100, maxfun // 8))))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_log_objective,
            z0,
            args=(float(beta),),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxls": 10,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        z = z0

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return score_full(a0), a0.copy(), maxfun

    sarr = softmax_pos(z)
    sarr = normalize_pos(sarr)
    sc = score_full(sarr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e50:
        return score_full(a0), a0.copy(), maxfun
    return sc, sarr, maxfun


def spectral_shave(a0, n_iters=200, alpha=0.5, target_start=0.98,
                   target_end=0.94, deadline=None):
    """
    Spectral amplitude update that tries to flatten coefficients of A(z)^2.
    """
    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = score_full(a)
    best_a = a.copy()

    m = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    fails = 0

    for it in range(int(n_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = fast_conv(a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mval = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * mval
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best:
            best = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        prog = float(it) / max(1, int(n_iters))
        thr = mval * (float(target_start) + (float(target_end) - float(target_start)) * prog)
        ct = c.copy()
        over = ct > thr
        if np.any(over):
            ct[over] = thr + 0.25 * (ct[over] - thr)

        csum = float(ct.sum())
        if csum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(csum):
            break
        ct /= csum

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
        C_old = A * A
        C_des = np.fft.rfft(ct, m_fft)

        den = np.abs(C_old)
        den = np.where(den > 1e-30, den, 1e-30)
        ratio = np.sqrt(np.abs(C_des) / den)
        ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.0, 5.0)

        A_new = A * ((1.0 - alpha) + alpha * ratio)
        a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, m_fft)[:n]
        a_new = normalize_pos(a_new)
        sc_new = score_full(a_new)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < sc - 1e-12:
            a = a_new
            fails = 0
            if sc_new < best:
                best = sc_new
                best_a = a_new.copy()
        else:
            fails += 1
            if fails > 25:
                a = best_a.copy()
                fails = 0
                alpha = max(0.15, alpha * 0.75)

    return best, best_a


def peak_shave(a0, n_iters=1000, top_k=5, base_step=0.002, max_step=0.012,
               deadline=None):
    """
    Exact subgradient peak shaving for max coefficient of A(z)^2.
    Operates on the simplex sum(a)=1 and uses an active-set of top lags.
    """
    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = score_full(a)
    best_a = a.copy()

    step = float(base_step)
    max_step = float(max_step)
    lower = 2.0 * float(n) / (2.0 * float(n) - 1.0)
    fails = 0
    lag_len = 2 * n - 1

    for _ in range(int(n_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = fast_conv(a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mval = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * mval
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best:
            best = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if sc - lower < 1e-10:
            break

        tk = int(min(top_k, lag_len))
        if tk >= lag_len:
            idx = np.arange(lag_len, dtype=np.int64)
            weights = np.ones(tk, dtype=np.float64) / float(tk)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -tk)[-tk:]
            vals = c[idx]
            shifted = np.clip(2000.0 * (vals - mval), -700.0, 0.0)
            weights = np.exp(shifted)
            wsum = float(weights.sum())
            if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
                weights = np.ones(tk, dtype=np.float64) / float(tk)
            else:
                weights /= wsum

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for pos, kk in enumerate(idx):
            k = int(kk)
            w = float(weights[pos])
            if w == 0.0:
                continue
            j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j1 = min(k, n - 1)
            if j1 >= j0:
                grad[j0:j1 + 1] += 2.0 * w * a[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]

        g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break
        d = -g / gnorm

        accepted = False
        for trial in (step, step * 0.5, step * 0.25, step * 0.1):
            a_try = a + trial * d
            a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 0.0)
            s_try = float(a_try.sum())
            if s_try <= 1e-300:
                continue
            a_try = a_try / s_try
            sc_try = score_full(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < sc - 1e-13:
                a = a_try
                accepted = True
                fails = 0
                step = min(max_step, step * 1.07)
                if sc_try < best:
                    best = sc_try
                    best_a = a.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            step = max(1e-5, step * 0.72)
            fails += 1
            if fails > 45:
                a = best_a.copy()
                fails = 0

    return best, best_a


def random_coord_polish(a0, max_trials=300, rng=None, deadline=None):
    """Small random multiplicative perturbations on heavier coordinates."""
    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    n = a.size
    best = score_full(a)
    best_a = a.copy()

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)

    top = np.argpartition(a, -min(500, n))[-min(500, n):]

    for it in range(int(max_trials)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        idx = int(top[int(rng.integers(top.size))])
        f = float(np.exp(rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35)))
        a_try = a.copy()
        a_try[idx] *= f
        a_try = normalize_pos(a_try)
        sc = score_full(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best:
            best = sc
            best_a = a_try.copy()

    return best, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Entry point invoked by the grader.
    Returns a list of non-negative floats minimizing the evaluation function.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = min(float(budget_s), 2400.0)
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, time_limit - 60.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n_main = 10000

    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size >= 32:
                parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    print(f"Starting search, budget={time_limit:.0f}s, n_main={n_main}")

    seeds = seed_sweep(n_main, parent, rng)
    if not seeds:
        best_a = make_binomial(n_main, 1.0)
    else:
        best_score0, best_a0 = seeds[0]
        best_a = normalize_pos(best_a0.copy())

    best_score = score_full(best_a)
    print(f"Initial best: {best_score:.8f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # Spectral pre-shaping, shorter if parent is already strong.
    spectral_budget = 90.0 if best_score > 1.53 else 35.0
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - max(120.0, spectral_budget):
        spec_deadline = min(deadline - 240.0, time.monotonic() + spectral_budget)
        sc, a = spectral_shave(best_a, n_iters=500, alpha=0.55,
                               target_start=0.98, target_end=0.94,
                               deadline=spec_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            print(f"Spectral shave: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Main log-space LSE annealing.
    if best_score > 1.53:
        betas = [10.0, 40.0, 150.0, 600.0, 2000.0, 8000.0]
    else:
        betas = [40.0, 150.0, 600.0, 2000.0, 8000.0]

    a_work = best_a.copy()
    for beta in betas:
        now = time.monotonic()
        reserve = 300.0
        remaining = deadline - now - reserve
        if remaining < 45.0:
            break
        tb = min(180.0, 0.38 * max(30.0, remaining))
        sc, a2, nf = run_lbfgs(a_work, beta, tb, maxiter_cap=1800)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            print(f"LSE beta={beta:7.0f}: internal={sc:.8f} (n={a2.size}, fun~{nf})")
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a2.copy()
            if sc < best_score + 0.004:
                a_work = a2.copy()
            else:
                a_work = best_a.copy()
        else:
            a_work = best_a.copy()

    # Exact subgradient peak shaving, widening the active lag set.
    for tk, cap_iters, reserve in ((3, 2200, 360), (10, 1500, 250), (25, 900, 180)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = deadline - now - reserve
        if remaining < 25.0:
            break
        n_iters = max(300, min(cap_iters, int(remaining * 40.0 * (5.0 / float(tk + 2.0)))))
        phase_deadline = min(deadline - max(10.0, reserve - 5.0), time.monotonic() + remaining)
        sc, a2 = peak_shave(best_a, n_iters=n_iters, top_k=tk,
                            base_step=0.0018, max_step=0.010,
                            deadline=phase_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print(f"Peak shave top_k={tk}: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Smooth re-flattening after exact peak shaving.
    for beta in (3000.0, 12000.0):
        now = time.monotonic()
        reserve = 180.0
        remaining = deadline - now - reserve
        if remaining < 35.0:
            break
        tb = min(110.0, 0.45 * max(25.0, remaining))
        sc, a2, nf = run_lbfgs(best_a, beta, tb, maxiter_cap=1200)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print(f"Post-peak LSE beta={beta:.0f}: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Final exact shaving and light random polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0
        if remaining > 30.0:
            sc, a2 = peak_shave(best_a,
                                n_iters=int(min(1500, max(200, remaining * 35.0))),
                                top_k=5,
                                base_step=0.0012,
                                max_step=0.006,
                                deadline=deadline - 60.0)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a2.copy()
                print(f"Final peak shave: {best_score:.8f}")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40.0:
        sc, a2 = random_coord_polish(
            best_a,
            max_trials=int(min(800, max(100, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 35.0) * 120.0))),
            rng=rng,
            deadline=deadline - 35.0
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print(f"Random coordinate polish: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Controlled length expansion.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        current_n = best_a.size
        for new_n in (12000, 14000):
            if new_n <= current_n or new_n > 16000:
                continue
            now = time.monotonic()
            remaining = deadline - now - 90.0
            if remaining < 45.0:
                break

            big = interpolate_seq(best_a, new_n)
            sc_big = score_full(big)
            print(f"Interpolated to n={new_n}: {sc_big:.8f}")

            if sc_big < best_score - 1e-6 or sc_big < best_score + 0.0005:
                big_deadline = min(deadline - 80.0, now + min(90.0, remaining * 0.45))
                sc_big2, a_big = peak_shave(
                    big,
                    n_iters=int(min(800, max(200, (big_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 35.0))),
                    top_k=5,
                    base_step=0.0012,
                    max_step=0.006,
                    deadline=big_deadline
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc_big2) and sc_big2 < sc_big:
                    sc_big = sc_big2
                    big = a_big.copy()

                if sc_big < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_big
                    best_a = big.copy()
                    print(f"Length expansion improved: {best_score:.8f} at n={best_a.size}")

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0 and new_n == 12000:
                bnew = make_binomial(new_n, 1.0)
                sc_b = score_full(bnew)
                if sc_b < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_b
                    best_a = bnew.copy()

    if best_a is None or best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = make_binomial(10000, 1.0)
        best_score = score_full(best_a)

    best_a = normalize_pos(np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0))

    # Optional direct verification.  The internal score is exact for the
    # stated evaluator, but this helps detect environment-specific issues.
    try:
        if "evaluate_sequence" in globals() and best_a.size <= 14000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            tentative = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
            if sum(tentative) > 0.01:
                ver = evaluate_sequence(tentative)
                if np.isfinite(ver):
                    print(f"Verification: internal={best_score:.8f}, external={ver:.8f}, n={len(tentative)}")
                else:
                    print("Verification returned non-finite; using fallback.")
    except Exception:
        pass

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    if not out or sum(out) <= 0.01:
        out = [0.5, 0.5]

    final_time = time.monotonic() - t0
    print(f"FINAL internal score={best_score:.8f}, length={len(out)}, time={final_time:.1f}s")
    return out
```
